Ka ’T ien Lamphrang 

Shuwa ba ngan thoh ia kane ka ’tien lamphrang, la 
pynkynmaw ia nga ia ka jingkyntu jong ki arngut ki 
rangbah graduates mynta la bun snem, la palat 14-15 
snem. Uwei na kine u long u aamla graduate na ka tbain 
Sheila (Nongwar) uba kyrteng u Mr. Rosmanik Wriang, 
une um don shuh mynta. Une u sain la u la kyntu ia nga 
la ar sien, lai sien ‘Thoh kot noli, khnang ba kita ki kot 
ba phin thoh kin long jingkyrkhu ia ki briew kiba pule bad 
ban sah jingkynmaw ia phi.” I Rai Bahadur D. Ropmay 
ruh kumjuh i la kyntu ban thoh ka kot kaba ka subject kan 
long shaphang U Jisu Khrist. Kumta ka lynnong ba 
nyngkong ha kane ka kot, la ka long kaba lyngkot ka long 
shaphang U Jisu Khrist. 

Kine ki jingkyntu kine ki rangbah, ki long kiba khraw 
bad ba kordor, hynrei ha kita ki por ki hap kum ka umslap 
ha u mawsiaug plea plen — khlem ngam bad pynsngew 
shaniah ei ei ha ka jiugmut jong nga katba ngan sngewnud 
ban thoh kot ei ei. Kumta ngam shym shim ia la u 
khulom ban leh ia kaba kine ki rangbah ki la kyntu Ha

nga hi nga sngew ba ka long ka kam kaba ngam lah ban 
leh. Kumta la shu klet ha ka jingbym sngew lah ban leh 
kat ban kynjoh sha ka kam thoh kot. Kumta ki Mss. 
baroh ym lah shem shuh bad ki bynta ba la pule ha ki 
kot hangne liangtai ruh ym lah shem shuh 

Dei haba uwei u samla graduate bad ki Editors jong 
ka Pateng Khristan bad Jingkyrsiew ki la pan ban shon 
ia ki jingkren jong nga, ia kiba teng teng nga la ai, la 
pynkyndit jingpyrkhat ba nga dei ban sumar ia ki bad 
thoh pynbha pat ia ki, khnang ba ngan ioh lum noh ia 
ki ha ka kot. Kumta u khulom jong nga u la sdang 
sngewtynnad. 

Hynrei ban jied ia ka kyrteng ngam pat lah. Ki 
jingkyrkhu ba nga la mad, kiba wan lyngba ka jingjairjar 
jong ka mfet ki shon ha ka jingmut jong nga ba nga dei 



(iv) 

ban jied da kane ka kyrteng “Ha ka jingjarjar jong ka 
miet,” khnang ba kane ka kyrteng kan long rub ka 
jingkyrkhu, ka ban bat pynkynmaw ia ka jingkordor kaba 
don ha ka. 

Kura u nongkren nalor ka pulpit, ba u paMbah ba bun, 
nga la dei ban kren bad ban ialeh ban pyashai noh ia la ka 
jingkren ba kajuh ka khyllipmat, da kaba pynbud ruh ia ki 
illustrations kiba lah ban pynsbai bad ban pynkynduh 
ruh ia ki nongsngap bad nongpule shaphang ka Pop, ka 
Hok bad ka Jingbishar de, da ka bor U Mynsiem U Blei. 
Kawei pa kawei ka raat jingkren ka don ki illustrations. 
Ia kajuh ka Illustration lah ban pynphai bad ring jingmut

da kumwei pat ha kawei pat ka jingkren, nalor kane ba 
nga pyndonkam. Kajuh ka lyntang siang kaba shi inshi 
shiteng, lah ban pyndonkam ha kiwei kiwei pat ki jingshfta 
ba bun rukom, ha ki kti u misteri uba don ki tiar kiba nep. 
U Spurgeon u ong ba u lah ban pyndonkam ia kajuh ka 
illustration ha kiwei kiwei ki bynts ba u kren. 

Nga ainguh ia U Blei ba U la ai bor bad lad ia nga, ba 
nga lah ban thoh ia kane ka kot. Nga sngewnguh ruh ia 
kito ki lok kiba la kyntu bad pynshlur ia nga ban leh 
la kane, ym tang da ka ktien, hynrei kiba la eei ia la 
ka kti ban iarap haduh ka jinglut jingsep, kum ia I 
Mr. D. Lyngdoh bad kiwei de 

U Blei hi Un kyrkhu da la U Mynsiem ka Jingkular, 

Amen. 


Bated Lawbah, 
Mawsynrara P 0.

D. GALYNE. 



IN THE STILL WATCHES OF THE NIGHT 


It is indeed a great pleasure to commend to young and 
old alike this volume of sermons and addresses by this yet 
another beloved disciple of our Lord . I believe many will be 
helped in their witness and in their Christian learning as

disciples by a study of this volume. As we read it we can 
discover some of Rev. D. Qalyne’s qualities as a preacher of 
the Word of God, although it will still be necessary for us to 
know the man and to listen to his voice before we can realize 
fully the degree of his fidelity to the Word which has been 
entrusted to him. 

Let me mention a few of the qualities which come to light 
f rom a reading of these addresses 

L The complete sincerity with which he sees and hears 
God's wonder full works and testifies of them. 

2. The absence of flattery. He wastes not a single word 
in flattering people He can encourage men without attempt - 
ing to flatter them . This is one of his characteristics . Lhave 
often heard him during the past ten years dealing with the 
problems of the depression in the border area ; and guiding 
his people with true insight to profit from their many afflic- 
tions. Such courageous declaration of God's living Word is 
the very essence of preaching | and I wish there had been more 
of just this among our Welsh people in this generation . 

3 It becomes clear that he loves preaching , especially the 
kind of preaching which on the continent of Europe is known 
as Evangelical and Confessional preaching as a function of 
the life of the Confessional Church. 

4. As we read these addresses we can see how carefully 
he listens to the Word as it is being given to him and strives to 
discern its implications , how diligently he endeavours to apply 
it to the spiritual needs of the people , how assiduously he 
collects illustrative material from a wide field t and how

(Vi) 


skilfully he uses his illustrations not merely to illustrate a 
point but also to involve the attention and interests of his 
listeners in the issues with which he is dealing . A study of 
his address on the needs of a minister , which he delivered in 
Wahiajer in 1950 help us to know the source of his resources . 

May Ood continue to bless him and his messages . 

T. Bevan Phillips , 
Theological College, Cherrapunji*

HA KA JINGJAT JAB JOHG KA MIST

Hynrei peit pat shaphang ka bor ba imat kumba shah 
jop. Shisien peit la imat kumta da ka jingbishar briew, 
ba khlem ka bor jingpynshai kaba da U Mynsiem Bakhuid

shem ia ka bor ba phylla ka jingtrei ka bor U Blei ha 
la kiba ngeit. Kumta ka jiogim joog ki ka jphla bad 
sakhi. 


Ha kawei ka spah snem ba la dep, la don ka jingkhih 
ba shyrkhei jong ki Boxers ha China. Kice ki Boxers ki 
la pyniap beifi shibun ia ki Khristan ha China. Ha kawei 
ka sngi ki la ker tawiar ia kawei ka iingskul Mission. Ki 
la kbang bad keikut ia ki khyrdop baroh, bad ki pyllait 
sa tang kawei. Ki la weng bad shim ia ka diengphna 
bad ki la buh pynthiah ha khmat kata ka khyrdop ba ki 
la pyllait ban mih. 


Te ki la ai kumne ka hukum, ba uno uno lane kano 
kano ki ban iuh ia kane ka diengphna, yn pyllait im ia 
ki, hynrei ia ki bym inh iaka, yn siat iap ia ki. Kumta 
la pynieng ha u line 100 ngut. La pyllait ter sLiter. Ka- 
ba nyngkong dei ka khynnah kynthei ka mih bak bak ka 
iuh thrait ia ka diengphna. Ki shim ia ka ki da buh 
kyrpang ha ka phang lait im. Kumta ter ter la bud

kiwei pat ki khynnah shynrang bad kynthei dap 7 ngut, 
kine ruh ki la iuh thrait is ka diengphna, bad kumta la 
pynieng bad pynithuh ba kine ki la ieng ha ka phang 
bad ha u line lait im. La pynmih sa kawei pat ka 
khynnah kynthei, kane pat kam shym bud kat ban iuh ia 
ka diengphna, hynrei ka la dem khohsiew bad nguh ngon ia 
ka diengphna, kumba duwai de artad. Kumta ka la ieng, 
Bad la ialam ia ka sha ka jaka ba dei ban siat. Bad la 
siat ia ka khnang ban pynshyrkhei ia kita kiwei. Hynrei 
kita baroh kiba sah ba ieng ha u line kim sheptieng wat 
haba ki la iohi ba la siat ia ka. Baroh ki la bud ia 
kane ka khynnah kynthei kaba shlur. Ki da dem khoh- 
siew ngon ha ka diengphna. Bad kumta la siat shanmari
